# v0.2.28 release evidence

The release uses the already accepted main-source commit `71ebedd173aab089260f9085adc581cd9c20f1d4`. Later main commits update documentation and research disposition; the complete engine source closure in the downloaded CI artifact matches the current checkout. No rebuilt executable replaces the CI artifact.

The existing complete native battery runs against those CI bytes. After publication, the public archive and checksum are compared with the preserved CI artifact, GitHub provenance is verified, and the unchanged public installer is exercised in an isolated installation directory. The existing installed-release suite runs with explicit Auto quantization, a ten-GB adaptive ceiling, the declared context and draft settings. User installation and shell profiles are not changed. These are functional and distribution checks, not new throughput measurements or qualification of other Macs or experimental quantizations.

Each text artifact below is stored byte-for-byte using zlib/base64 and verified by a decode round trip. Generated tensor/binary archives are excluded; their paths, sizes and hashes are retained in the inventory. The published binary, Metal library and build source archive remain independently identified in the build identities and attested release archive.
